- **Lot 042 (liaison validation, hiérarchie des titres, parité `container-fluid` Import/Export, done — 2026-07-27, ticket doc `docs/tickets-tdd-lot-042-validation-titres-parite-editeurs.md`)**: closes the last 3 retained findings from `audit-design-blazoradmin-2026-07-27.md`/`audit-priorisation-design-blazoradmin-2026-07-27.md` — color contrast stays explicitly out of scope (not calculable statically, needs a real-browser check). Implemented 42.3 → 42.1 → 42.2 per the ticket's own recommended order.
  - **42.3**: `ImportProfileEditor.razor` gained the same `<div class="container-fluid px-3">` wrapper as `ExportProfileEditor.razor` — 42.0's investigation (git blame on the `container-fluid px-3` addition, commit `7f89b50`, Lot X's X11) found **no functional reason** for the divergence: X6 (Lot X) scoped the fix only to the export screenshot the client had reported at the time, and it was simply never revisited for the import twin in the same commit. Confirmed via a dedicated bUnit test mirroring `ExportProfileEditorTests.cs`'s own pre-existing `RootContainer_HasContainerFluidWithPadding`.
  - **42.1**: `Login.razor`/`Register.razor`/`Profile.razor` — every field with a `<ValidationMessage>` gained a matching `aria-describedby`. **Real, empirically-confirmed gap in the ticket's own assumption**: Blazor's `ValidationMessage<TValue>` renders **nothing at all** (not even an empty `<div>`) when the field has no validation error — confirmed by a failing bUnit `ElementNotFoundException` before the fix — so an `id` on `ValidationMessage` itself is not a reliable `aria-describedby` target from initial render. Fixed by wrapping each `<ValidationMessage>` in its own always-present `<div id="{field}-validation">`, with `aria-describedby` pointing at that wrapper rather than at `ValidationMessage` directly. No change to Identity's underlying validation logic (scaffolded as-is, per the ticket's own explicit exclusion).
  - **42.2**: every heading-level skip the audit found was fixed by shifting the affected heading(s) down exactly one semantic level, preserving pre-existing visual size via a Bootstrap `.hN` utility class wherever the size wasn't already independently controlled by another class (mirrors the ticket's own `<h3 class="h5">` worked example) — `NotFound.razor` (`h3`→`h1`, genuinely missing before, no prior visual class to preserve); `ImportProfileEditor.razor`/`ExportProfileEditor.razor`'s section headings (`h3`→`h2 class="h3"`) and sheet-rule-card titles (`h4`→`h3 class="sheet-rule-card-title h4"`); `ImportProfileEditor.razor`'s nested unconditional-colonnes/point-rules sub-headings (`h5`→`h4 class="h5"`); `SheetRuleForm.razor`/`SheetGenerationRuleForm.razor`'s own section headings (`h5`→`h3 class="h5"`, siblings of the page's `h2`, not nested under the card-title `h3` — neither the always-present "Add" card nor the in-place edit mode ever wraps them in an intermediate title); the 3 test pages' rejected/technical-error headings (`ImportProfileTest.razor`/`ExportProfileTest.razor`/`ApiTest.razor`, `h4`→`h2 class="h4"`, the only headings on those pages besides the page's own `h1` — every other section title on those pages is a `<summary>`, not a heading, so out of scope). **Found beyond the ticket's own minimal file list, per its explicit "confirm no other unlisted skip exists" instruction**: `ImportProfiles.razor`/`ExportProfiles.razor`/`GeneratedFiles.razor`/`Users.razor`'s mobile-card per-row titles (`h5 class="card-title"`→`h2 class="card-title h5"`) — Bootstrap's `.card-title` only sets margin, never font-size, so the `h5` utility class was needed there too to keep the row title's visual size unchanged. New shared `HeadingHierarchyAssertions.AssertNoHeadingLevelSkip` (`tests/ExcelETL.BlazorAdmin.Tests/Pages/HeadingHierarchyAssertions.cs`, same one-file-shared-by-many-test-classes precedent as `FormFloatingStructureAssertions`, Lot 030) checks exactly-one-`h1`-first plus no level jump greater than +1 anywhere after it; applied to one representative render per corrected page (including the rejected/technical-error branch for the 3 test pages, reusing their existing fixture-seeding helpers). 2 pre-existing `ImportProfileEditorTests.cs` assertions that queried `h5` directly for the (now-`h4`) sub-headings were updated to query `h4` — a deliberate, not-vacuous fix (their sibling `BeEmpty()` assertions were re-verified to still exercise real absence, not an accidental pass from the tag rename).
  - Full `ExcelETL.BlazorAdmin.Tests` suite: 510/510 green (was 495 before this lot; +1 `NotFoundTests`, +1 `ImportProfileEditorTests` container-fluid, +20 across the heading-hierarchy/aria-describedby additions, +6 `LoginTests`/`RegisterTests`/`ProfileTests` aria-describedby). Full solution build green.
  - **Hors périmètre, per the ticket**: color contrast (explicitly excluded by Simon, 28/07 — not calculable statically, `--m3-*` variables recompose via `color-mix()` at runtime under light/dark theme); any further visual harmonization between the two profile editors beyond the one `container-fluid` divergence; any redesign of heading size/weight/color beyond the semantic `hN` level itself; the Identity scaffolding's underlying validation logic.
- **Lot 043 (confirmation de navigation avant perte de modifications non enregistrées, done — 2026-07-27, ticket doc `docs/tickets-tdd-lot-043-confirmation-navigation-modifications-non-enregistrees.md`)**: `ImportProfileEditor.razor`/`ExportProfileEditor.razor` still only persist on an explicit Save click (unchanged, not reopened by this lot) — the risk closed here is purely UX: navigating away via a sidebar link previously discarded in-progress edits with zero warning. Implemented 43.0 → 43.1 → 43.2/43.3 (grouped in the same commit per the ticket's own note) in the recommended order.
  - **43.0 investigation, real findings that shaped the implementation**: neither editor uses an `EditForm`/`EditContext` at all — every root field is a plain `@bind` on a bare `<input>`, no Blazor validation wrapper — so the ticket's assumed `EditContext.OnFieldChanged` hook doesn't exist; used `@bind:after="MarkAsChanged"` on each root field instead (a native .NET 7+ feature, equally simple, no new abstraction). The already-existing mutation entry points (`HandleAddSheetRule`/`HandleSaveSheetRule`/`DeleteSheetRule`/`ConfirmDeleteSheetRule` and the 4 default-Tableau/Application-name Add/Save/Delete methods) needed one `MarkAsChanged()` call added each, no new event plumbing. **`<NavigationLock>` is fully testable end-to-end in bUnit**: confirmed by a spike test that bUnit's `TestNavigationManager` — a plain `NavigationManager` subclass — inherits the base class's own location-changing-handler pipeline (added in .NET 8 specifically so this works uniformly across every hosting model, test doubles included), so `NavigationManager.NavigateTo(...)` triggers `OnBeforeInternalNavigation` synchronously exactly like a real browser; no manually-constructed `LocationChangingContext` fallback was needed. The delete-confirmation banner pattern (`ImportProfiles.razor`/`ExportProfiles.razor`, an `@if`-swapped message + 2 actions) was reused for the navigation-confirmation UI rather than inventing a new design.
  - **43.1/43.2 (`ImportProfileEditor.razor`/`ExportProfileEditor.razor`, mirrored)**: a single `_hasUnsavedChanges` bool (no per-field tracking, no diffing — the ticket's own explicit KISS/YAGNI call), reset to `false` only after a *successful* `SaveAsync` (an exception leaves it `true`, so the warning keeps applying on a failed save). `<NavigationLock ConfirmExternalNavigation="_hasUnsavedChanges" OnBeforeInternalNavigation="OnBeforeInternalNavigationAsync" />` is always rendered (harmless no-op when clean). `OnBeforeInternalNavigationAsync` calls `context.PreventNavigation()` + stores `context.TargetLocation` + shows an inline `#unsaved-changes-navigation-confirmation` banner only when dirty. `#discard-changes-and-leave-button` clears `_hasUnsavedChanges` **before** calling `NavigationManager.NavigateTo(target)` — required so the lock's own handler (still registered, now permanently harmless) doesn't re-block the very navigation it just approved; `#stay-on-page-button` only closes the banner, no state reset. Same IDs on both editors per the ticket's explicit parity requirement.
  - **43.3 (indicator)**: `#unsaved-changes-indicator` (`text-warning` span, no new CSS class) renders next to the Save button whenever `_hasUnsavedChanges` is true — folded into the same commit as 43.1/43.2 per the ticket's own "trivial, can be grouped" note, not a separate cycle.
  - **Real, non-obvious finding surfaced only by the 43.2 parity test**: mounting both editors simultaneously against one shared `NavigationManager` (as the parity test initially did) breaks — the real .NET `NavigationManager` invokes registered location-changing handlers *sequentially* and stops at the first one that calls `PreventNavigation()`, so a second component's own handler never even runs if an earlier-registered one (still dirty) already blocked the navigation. Fixed in the test by clicking import's own `#discard-changes-and-leave-button` (clearing its dirty flag, making its handler a permanent no-op) before rendering the export side — never an issue in the real app, since only one of the two editors is ever mounted at a time, but a genuine bUnit-testing gotcha worth remembering for any future test mounting two `NavigationLock`-bearing components against one `TestNavigationManager`.
  - New resx keys, both editors (EN/FR, page-prefixed per this project's established per-page convention even though the wording is identical): `*_UnsavedChangesIndicator`, `*_UnsavedChangesConfirmMessage`, `*_DiscardChangesAndLeaveButton`, `*_StayOnPageButton`.
  - Tests: `ImportProfileEditorTests.cs` 142/142 green (+13 vs. before this lot), `ExportProfileEditorTests.cs` 108/108 green (+10), `ProfileEditorParityTests.cs` gained 1 dedicated structural-parity test (NavigationLock presence/state + confirmation-banner/discard/stay button class strings, string-compared per the file's established convention). Full `ExcelETL.BlazorAdmin.Tests` suite: 534/534 green. Full solution build green.
  - **Hors périmètre, per the ticket**: auto-save; per-field dirty tracking; a structured before/after diff; a new shared Blazor component factoring the two editors beyond what already exists (duplication accepted, consistent with the project's own precedent); extension to `Login.razor`/`Register.razor`/`Profile.razor`/`ImportProfileTest.razor`/`ExportProfileTest.razor`; customizing the native browser dialog behind `ConfirmExternalNavigation` (not possible from Blazor, accepted as-is).
- **Lot 044 (gestion des utilisateurs — création/modification/suppression/reset mot de passe, done — 2026-07-27, ticket doc `docs/tickets-tdd-lot-044-gestion-utilisateurs.md`)**: closes the client's deployment-driven need to create/remove other accounts himself rather than sharing his own credentials. `Users.razor` was previously read-only. No new role — "non-admin" is simply an account with no role at all; the create form offers no role choice, so an Admin can never be created from the UI.
  - **44.2 (migration)**: `ApplicationUser` gains `bool RequirePasswordChangeOnFirstLogin` (default `false`, 3 seeded accounts unaffected), migration `20260727130533_AddRequirePasswordChangeOnFirstLoginToApplicationUser` (Identity migrations folder, `src/ExcelETL.Infrastructure/Identity/Migrations/`). Generated via `dotnet ef migrations add ... --project src/ExcelETL.Infrastructure --startup-project src/ExcelETL.WebAPI --context ApplicationIdentityDbContext` — WebAPI works as the startup project here (has `Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Design`, BlazorAdmin doesn't) even though it never resolves `ApplicationIdentityDbContext` itself; `dotnet ef` resolves it through `ApplicationIdentityDbContextFactory` (design-time factory), not host DI.
  - **44.1 (`IUserManagementService`/`UserManagementService`)**: new Application/Infrastructure pair, `src/ExcelETL.Application/Identity/IUserManagementService.cs` + `src/ExcelETL.Infrastructure/Identity/UserManagementService.cs` — `AddScoped` in `ExcelETL.BlazorAdmin/Program.cs`, right next to `IUserRepository`. `CreateUserAsync`/`ResetPasswordAsync` both generate a temporary password via a new `TemporaryPasswordGenerator` (`Infrastructure/Identity/`, cryptographically random via `RandomNumberGenerator`, 12 chars, guarantees at least one upper/lower/digit/special character — safely exceeds ASP.NET Core Identity's default complexity policy, confirmed at 44.0 that `IdentityOptions.Password` is never customized anywhere in this solution) and set `RequirePasswordChangeOnFirstLogin = true`. `ResetPasswordAsync` uses the standard `GeneratePasswordResetTokenAsync` + `ResetPasswordAsync(user, token, newPassword)` token flow (no prior password needed) — confirmed at 44.0 there was no pre-existing Remove/AddPassword usage anywhere to mirror. `DeleteUserAsync(userId, currentUserId)` refuses **explicitly** (a `UserDeletionResult` with `UserDeletionFailureReason.SelfDeletion`/`LastAdminRemaining`, never an exception) for self-deletion or for the sole remaining `Admin` (via `UserManager.GetUsersInRoleAsync(IdentitySeeder.AdminRoleName).Count == 1`); self-check runs before any `FindByIdAsync`/`DeleteAsync` call. `GetAdminUserIdsAsync()` lets the UI pre-compute the "last Admin" guard client-side to disable the delete button proactively, without needing a real deletion attempt to be refused first. **Deliberately does not duplicate `IUserRepository.UpdateProfileAsync`** (firstName/lastName/email edit, no password/role change) — `Users.razor`'s "modify" action reuses that pre-existing method as-is, confirmed at 44.0 there's no equivalent already covering create/reset/delete that this could extend instead. Tests (`UserManagementServiceTests`/`TemporaryPasswordGeneratorTests`, Infrastructure.Tests) mock `UserManager<ApplicationUser>` via the pre-existing `IdentityManagerMocks.CreateUserManagerMock()` helper — same convention as `IdentitySeederTests`/`UserRepositoryTests`, no `RoleManager` needed since every role check used here (`IsInRoleAsync`/`GetUsersInRoleAsync`) is a `UserManager` method.
  - **44.3 (`Users.razor`)**: on the model of `ImportProfiles.razor`/`ExportProfiles.razor` (Lot 028) — icon-only row actions (`AdminIconMarkup.Pencil`/a new `AdminIconMarkup.Key` for reset-password/`AdminIconMarkup.Trash`), inline confirmation banners (`role="alert"`, `Confirm`/`Cancel`), duplicated between the desktop `<table>` and the mobile `d-md-none` card per this project's established V2 convention. Exactly one interactive sub-state (create form / row edit / row reset-password confirm / row delete confirm) is active across the *whole page* at a time (`ClearAllTransientState()` called at the start of every "request X" method) — generalizes Lot 028's "one confirmation at a time" rule to every action this lot adds, not just delete. `#temporary-password-display` (a `bg-success-subtle` card, `role="alert"`) shows the just-generated password once, with an explicit "never shown again" warning and a dismiss button — shared by both the create and reset-password success paths. The delete button is rendered `disabled` (not merely hidden) with an explicit `aria-label`/`title` reason whenever `CanDelete(user)` is false (current user, or the sole remaining Admin per `GetAdminUserIdsAsync()`) — avoids a round trip to a server call already known to be refused. `IUserRepository.GetByIdAsync` is called on "modify" click to prefill first/last name (`UserSummary`, the list's own DTO, doesn't carry them). Tests (`UsersTests.cs`, bUnit): `@bind:event="oninput"` fields need `.Input(...)`, not `.Change(...)`, in bUnit — using `.Change()` on an `oninput`-bound element throws `Bunit.MissingEventHandlerException`.
  - **44.4 (`NavMenu.razor`)**: `#nav-logs-link` moved from the generic `<AuthorizeView>` (any authenticated user, Lot S's decision) into the existing `<AuthorizeView Roles="Admin">` block — a non-admin user created by this lot no longer sees Journaux in the sidebar. `Logs.razor`'s own `@attribute [Authorize]` (not `Roles = Admin`) is untouched — out of this ticket's literal scope, which only asked for the nav-link move, not route-level enforcement; a non-admin could still reach `/logs` by direct URL if that's ever revisited. `NavMenuTests.cs`'s `AdminLinkIds` array gained `"nav-logs-link"`; the pre-existing `NavMenu_WhenAuthorized_WithoutAdminRole_ShowsLogsLink` test was fixed in place (renamed `..._HidesLogsLink`, assertion inverted) rather than duplicated, per the ticket's own instruction.
  - **44.5 (resx)**: all new `Users_*` keys (EN+FR) audited for orphans post-hoc — one (`Users_EditFormHeading`) had been added but not yet referenced; used as the inline edit-form's own heading (table and mobile card) rather than removed, since removing would have meant a create/edit asymmetry (create form already has `Users_CreateFormHeading`).
  - Full solution after Lot 044: Domain 278 (unaffected)/Application 152 (unaffected)/Infrastructure 170 (was 152, +18: `UserManagementServiceTests` ×16, `TemporaryPasswordGeneratorTests` ×3 minus the pre-existing net, `ApplicationUserRequirePasswordChangeOnFirstLoginTests` ×2)/WebAPI 25 (unaffected)/Hosting 6 (unaffected)/legacy 9+15 (unaffected)/BlazorAdmin 548 (was 534, +14: `UsersTests` net +14 vs. its pre-Lot-044 4 tests, `NavMenuTests` net 0 — one test fixed in place, not added). Full solution 1203 tests, all green.
  - **Hors périmètre, per the ticket**: any new role (`User`, `NonAdmin`); Admin creation/promotion from the UI; email delivery of the temporary password (no SMTP infrastructure in this stack); role change on an existing user; bulk delete; any `NavMenu.razor` reorganization beyond the Logs-link move (Lot S's link order stays as-is); a dedicated audit table for user create/delete events (`SystemLogs` already captures generic application events via Serilog — no ticket requested a dedicated table, same reasoning already applied to the retired `ExtractionHistory`).
- **Lot 045 (application effective du verrou `RequirePasswordChangeOnFirstLogin`, done — 2026-07-27, ticket doc `docs/tickets/tickets-tdd-lot-045-application-verrou-mot-de-passe-temporaire.md`)**: closes the gap the Lot 044/état-des-lieux audit flagged as the sole real pre-deployment blocker — the flag was set at account creation/reset but nothing in the login flow or the app's navigation ever read it, so a temporary password stayed valid indefinitely until the user spontaneously visited `/profile`.
  - **45.0 investigation, confirmed before writing any test**: `Login.razor`/`Register.razor`/the new `ForcePasswordChange.razor` all live under `Components/Account/Pages/`, whose `_Imports.razor` carries `@attribute [ExcludeFromInteractiveRouting]` — these render as true static SSR regardless of the app's global `<Routes @rendermode="InteractiveServer">` (`App.razor`), so a server-side redirect and (for the new page) a direct in-POST auth-cookie rewrite are both safe there, unlike `Profile.razor`'s `InteractiveServer` password form (which still needs its pre-existing manual logout/relogin dance — untouched, not reopened). **Option A retained** (claim on the principal, not a per-navigation DB read): no `IUserClaimsPrincipalFactory<ApplicationUser>` existed yet. Refresh mechanism: `SignInManager.RefreshSignInAsync(user)`, callable directly from the static `ForcePasswordChange.razor` POST (impossible from `Profile.razor`'s interactive circuit, which is exactly why that page never adopted it). **Empirically confirmed while building 45.4**: `RendererInfo` throws `MissingRendererInfoException` unless a render mode is assigned to the component — a static-SSR `NavigationManager` also throws `NotSupportedException` on `RegisterLocationChangingHandler` (`<NavigationLock>`'s registration point) — so the guard's `<NavigationLock>` is only rendered behind `@if (RendererInfo.IsInteractive)`.
  - **45.1 (`UserRepository.ChangePasswordAsync`, `src/ExcelETL.Infrastructure/Identity/UserRepository.cs`)**: after a successful `UserManager.ChangePasswordAsync`, lifts the flag (`user.RequirePasswordChangeOnFirstLogin = false; await userManager.UpdateAsync(user);`) — but only when it was actually `true`, to avoid an unneeded extra `UpdateAsync`/security-stamp churn on the common (already-`false`) case. Never lifted on failure. Reused as-is by both `Profile.razor` (unchanged call site) and the new `ForcePasswordChange.razor` — no duplicated flag-reset logic anywhere.
  - **`RequirePasswordChangeClaimsPrincipalFactory`** (`src/ExcelETL.Infrastructure/Identity/`, `UserClaimsPrincipalFactory<ApplicationUser, IdentityRole>` override) adds a `RequirePasswordChangeOnFirstLogin` claim (`ClaimType` const, value `bool.TrueString`) whenever `user.RequirePasswordChangeOnFirstLogin` is `true`; absent otherwise. Registered via `.AddClaimsPrincipalFactory<...>()` chained onto the existing `AddIdentity<ApplicationUser, IdentityRole>(...)` in `ExcelETL.BlazorAdmin/Program.cs`. Regenerated on every `SignInManager`-built principal (login, `RefreshSignInAsync`), so the claim disappears the moment the flag is lifted and a fresh sign-in/refresh happens — no separate cache-invalidation mechanism needed.
  - **45.2 (`ForcePasswordChange.razor`, route `/Account/ForcePasswordChange`, `[Authorize]`, no role restriction)**: `OnInitializedAsync` resolves the current user via `UserManager.GetUserAsync(HttpContext.User)` (`[CascadingParameter] HttpContext HttpContext`, framework-supplied during the SSR request, same mechanism `StatusMessage.razor` already relies on) and redirects home immediately if the flag is already `false` — a user who doesn't need this page never sees the form. Reuses `IUserRepository.ChangePasswordAsync` (45.1) for the actual change; on success, re-fetches the user (now flag-`false`) and calls `SignInManager.RefreshSignInAsync(refreshedUser)` to rewrite the auth cookie with the claim gone, then redirects home — all inside the same static-SSR POST, no manual logout step. On failure, shows the raw Identity error via the existing `<StatusMessage>` component (same as `Profile.razor`), flag untouched. IDs exactly as specified by the ticket: `#force-password-change-form`/`#current-password-input`/`#new-password-input`/`#confirm-password-input`/`#force-password-change-submit`.
  - **45.3 (`Login.razor`)**: after a successful `PasswordSignInAsync`, looks up the user via the newly-injected `UserManager<ApplicationUser>.FindByNameAsync(Input.UserName)` and redirects to `ForcePasswordChange.Route` instead of `ReturnUrl` when the flag is `true` — `ReturnUrl` is silently dropped in that case (the user resumes their intended destination only after the forced change, via the guard's normal post-change navigation, not by round-tripping `ReturnUrl` through the change page). A failed sign-in is completely unaffected (`UserManager.FindByNameAsync` is never even called on that path).
  - **45.4 (`PasswordChangeGuard.razor`, `src/ExcelETL.BlazorAdmin/Components/Layout/`, rendered unconditionally at the very top of `MainLayout.razor`)**: two complementary checks, both reading the claim off the cascading `AuthenticationState` (no DB read) — `OnInitializedAsync` (fires on every fresh static SSR render *and* the first render of a new interactive circuit, covering a hard navigation/typed URL) and, only when `RendererInfo.IsInteractive`, `<NavigationLock OnBeforeInternalNavigation="...">` (covering a same-circuit SPA navigation, e.g. a sidebar `NavLink` click — confirmed working end-to-end under bUnit's `TestNavigationManager` per the same feasibility Lot 043 already established). Both funnel into one `RedirectIfRequiredAsync` that no-ops unless the claim is present, exempts `account/forcepasswordchange` and `account/logout` (case-insensitive path-prefix match) to avoid a redirect loop, and otherwise calls `context?.PreventNavigation()` (internal-navigation path only) + `NavigationManager.NavigateTo(ForcePasswordChange.Route)`.
  - **45.5 (resx)**: 3 new keys (`ForcePasswordChange_Title`/`_Explanation`/`_Submit`, EN+FR) — every field label/placeholder on the new page reuses `Profile_CurrentPasswordLabel`/`Profile_NewPasswordLabel`/`Profile_ConfirmNewPasswordLabel` (+ their `*Placeholder` counterparts) verbatim, per the ticket's own "reuse generic keys" instruction.
  - **Non-regression, explicitly tested**: `LoginTests.cs` gained a `FindByNameAsync` default setup returning `RequirePasswordChangeOnFirstLogin = false` so all 4 pre-existing tests (predating this lot) keep their exact prior redirect-to-`ReturnUrl` behavior with zero assertion changes; `LoginFormFloatingAuditTests` (Lot 030) needed only the new `UserManager` DI registration, no assertion changes. `MainLayoutTests.cs` needed one addition (`SetRendererInfo(new RendererInfo("Server", isInteractive: true))`, since `MainLayout` now renders `PasswordChangeGuard`) — both pre-existing culture-chrome tests otherwise unmodified.
  - New tests: `RequirePasswordChangeClaimsPrincipalFactoryTests`/3 flag-lift tests appended to `UserRepositoryTests` (Infrastructure.Tests); `ForcePasswordChangeTests`, 3 new `LoginTests` (flag-true redirect, flag-false non-regression, failed-login-never-redirects), `PasswordChangeGuardTests` (6 facts: admin-page redirect with claim, no redirect on the change page itself, no redirect on logout, no redirect without the claim, and both the "fresh render already on a blocked page" and "fresh render already on the allowed page" cases) — all in `ExcelETL.BlazorAdmin.Tests`. Full solution after Lot 045: Domain 278/Application 152/WebAPI 25/Hosting 6/legacy 9+15 (all unaffected) / Infrastructure 175 (was 170, +5) / BlazorAdmin 561 (was 548, +13) — all green.
  - **Hors périmètre, per the ticket**: any new password policy (complexity/expiration/history); email notification (no SMTP infra, consistent with Lot 044); account lockout/2FA; the temporary-password generation/one-time-display mechanism itself (Lot 044, unchanged); applying the lock to `/api/oxo/process` (API-key-protected, no Identity user account involved, explicitly out of scope).
- **Lot 046 (nettoyage de dette — retrait de `IFileStorageService` et de `IWorkbookReader.SheetExists`, done — 2026-07-27, ticket doc `docs/tickets/tickets-tdd-lot-046-nettoyage-filestorageservice-sheetexists.md`)**: pure debt reduction, arbitrated with Simon at the 2026-07-27 état-des-lieux review — no observable HTTP/business behavior change.
  - **46.0 investigation confirmed both removals were safe**: `IFileStorageService`'s only production caller was `ProcessOxoFileService` (the flat, metadata-less, target-only archive from Lot K); no other page/controller/service consumed it. The one test relying on its flat-file output was `OxoProcessEndpointTests.Process_WithValidRequestAgainstC7401Fixture_...` (the `Directory.GetFiles(_archiveDirectory, "*.xlsx").Should().ContainSingle()` assertion Lot 034's own notes had already flagged as pinning this exact behavior) — updated, not left broken. For `SheetExists`: an exhaustive search confirmed zero callers across the 5 extraction services, Infrastructure, and WebAPI; `WorksheetNotFoundInWorkbookException` is thrown from `ClosedXmlWorkbookReader.ReadCellValue` itself (on `TryGetWorksheet` failure), never via `SheetExists` — so removing it changes nothing about the missing-sheet path, confirmed by the pre-existing `ReadCellValue_WithUnknownSheet_ThrowsWorksheetNotFoundInWorkbookException` test continuing to pass unmodified as the non-regression proof.
  - **46.1/46.2 (`IFileStorageService`, one combined commit per the ticket's own note that the two sub-tickets aren't independently meaningful)**: `ProcessOxoFileService` dropped the `IFileStorageService` constructor parameter and the flat `SaveAsync` call entirely — Lot 034's `IGeneratedFileWriter`/`IGeneratedFileArchiveStore` (source + target, `{yyyy}/{MM}` tree, millisecond-timestamped names, SQL metadata, `/generated-files` page) is now the sole archiving mechanism, since it already covered strictly more than the Lot K mechanism it replaces. Deleted outright: `IFileStorageService`/`LocalFileStorageService`/`FileStorageOptions` (and their own `LocalFileStorageServiceTests`), the `AddScoped<IFileStorageService, LocalFileStorageService>()` + `Configure<FileStorageOptions>(...)` registrations in `ExcelETL.WebAPI/Program.cs`, and the `FileStorage:ArchiveDirectory` section from both `appsettings.json`/`appsettings.Development.json` (BlazorAdmin never referenced any of this). `ProcessOxoFileServiceTests`/`OxoProcessEndpointTests` updated to drop the now-nonexistent mock/config/assertions — the flat-file assertion in the C7401 success test was removed outright (superseded by the already-existing Lot 034 archive-record test, `Process_WithValidRequestAgainstC7401Fixture_PersistsArchiveRecordAndBothFilesOnDisk`, which already asserts both files land on disk via the real mechanism).
  - **46.3 (`IWorkbookReader.SheetExists`, independent commit)**: removed from the interface and `ClosedXmlWorkbookReader`, plus its 2 dedicated tests (`SheetExists_WithExistingSheet_ReturnsTrue`/`_WithMissingSheet_ReturnsFalse`) — no mock configuration anywhere set it up, so no `Mock<IWorkbookReader>` call site needed touching.
  - Full solution after Lot 046: Domain 278 (unaffected)/Application 152 (unaffected, net — 4 setup blocks removed from `ProcessOxoFileServiceTests`, no count change since none were their own `[Fact]`)/Infrastructure 173 (was 175, −2: `LocalFileStorageServiceTests` deleted, `ClosedXmlWorkbookReaderTests`'s 2 `SheetExists` tests removed)/WebAPI 25 (unaffected — the C7401 flat-file assertion was removed from an existing test, not a whole test)/Hosting 6/legacy 9+15/BlazorAdmin 561 (all unaffected) — all green.
  - **Hors périmètre, per the ticket**: the Lot 034 archiving mechanism itself (untouched); retention/purge policy (unchanged, still out of scope); `/api/oxo/process`'s HTTP contract (unchanged — same 200/404/422/400 codes and bodies); the `DirectCell`/profile-driven-header-migration subject (explicitly not touched, tracked separately); the `CS8604` warning on `_profileNamePendingDeletion` (explicitly deferred to a lot that touches `ImportProfiles.razor`/`ExportProfiles.razor` directly).
- **Lot 047 (profile-driven header cells, `DirectCell` finally wired, done — 2026-07-27/28, ticket doc `docs/tickets/tickets-tdd-lot-047-extraction-entetes-profile-driven-directcell.md`, spec `docs/reference/spec-migration-entetes-profile-driven-directcell.md`)**: the "moteur" half (no UI — that's Lot 048) of moving PROCEDURE's `M2:O2`/`P2:Q2`/`R2:T2` and AJT/DIVERS' `N6` repère echo from hardcoded literals into the profile. **Invariant held**: all 3 real fixtures produce byte-identical `nomMAD`/`Designation`/repère-echo values before and after — confirmed by the pre-existing, unmodified `ProcedureExtractionServiceIntegrationTests`/`ImportPipelineOrchestratorIntegrationTests`/`DefaultProfileSeederPipelineIntegrationTests` all staying green throughout.
  - **47.0 investigation**: `R2:T2` is a genuine Excel `DateTime` cell — `ClosedXmlWorkbookReader` already renders it via `CultureInfo.InvariantCulture` (the Lot K1 bug fix) as a fixed-format string (`"dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm:ss"`/`"dd/MM/yyyy"`), which `ProcedureExtractionService.TryParseDate` already parsed against that same fixed format list — confirming, per spec §5, that only the **output** format needed to become profile-driven; input parsing stays a hardcoded format list (now centralized in `HeaderRuleResolver.DateInputFormats`, mirrored by `ProcedureExtractionService`'s own copy for `TacheMultiple.DateValidation`, a field this lot's `HeaderFieldRule` doesn't cover).
  - **47.1 (Domain, `src/ExcelETL.Domain/Extraction/Profile/`)**: `HeaderFieldRule` (`Name`/`Cell: DirectCell`/`StripReperePrefix`/`DateFormat`, both flags default-off) and `HeaderCompositeRule` (`Name`/`Template`, plus a public `PlaceholderNames()` regex helper reused by both the cross-validation below and the resolver's own defensive check). `SheetExtractionRule` gained `HeaderFields`/`HeaderComposites` as two **new required constructor parameters** (no default — same anti-hardcoding-friendly, no-magic-default convention as `ImportProfile.DefaultTableaux`, Lot U1) — every one of the ~30 call sites across the whole solution (`DefaultProfileSeeder`, every per-sheet service's tests, `SheetRuleForm.razor`, `EfImportProfileStoreTests`, orchestrator/generation integration tests) updated mechanically to pass `[], []` (or real rules, where the sheet needs them). **Cross-validation lives on `SheetExtractionRule`** (ticket's own recommendation): a composite referencing a placeholder with no matching `HeaderFieldRule.Name` on the same sheet throws `DomainRuleViolationException` (`SheetExtractionRule_HeaderCompositeReferencesUnknownField`) at construction time — the resolver's own placeholder substitution (47.2) still has a second, defensive `UnknownFieldReferenceException` throw for the same case, deliberately unreachable through the only real construction path, same "defense in depth, not duplicated coverage" precedent as `TextTransformEvaluator`'s `Concat`/`FieldRef`. **EF-materialization gotcha, confirmed empirically (not assumed from precedent)**: `HeaderFieldRule.Cell` (a **reference** to an owned type, not a collection) still could not be constructor-bound by EF Core — the exact same "Navigations to related entities... cannot be bound" restriction `RepeatingBlockLocator.Fields`/`SheetExtractionRule.PointRules` already document for collections turned out to apply to a single owned-type reference too — so `HeaderFieldRule` needed the identical private-parameterless-constructor-plus-reflection trick, a first for a *reference* property in this codebase (every prior instance was a list).
  - **47.2 (Application, `src/ExcelETL.Application/Extraction/Oxo/`)**: `IHeaderRuleResolver`/`HeaderRuleResolver` — `Resolve(IWorkbookReader, SheetExtractionRule, reperePrefix)` reads every `HeaderFieldRule.Cell`, applies `StripReperePrefix` (via the pre-existing `SubstringAfter`/`ITextTransformEvaluator`) then `DateFormat` (reformat via the fixed input-format list), and substitutes every `HeaderCompositeRule.Template`. Deliberately **policy-free about "required"**: returns `HeaderFieldResolution(Name, RawValue, Value, ErrorMessage)` per field (RawValue always present, even on failure) rather than deciding what "missing" means — that stays each sheet service's own business call, since PROCEDURE distinguishes blank-cell (`RequiredFieldMissing`) from prefix-mismatch/unparsable-date (`UnparsableValue`) while AJT/DIVERS' `repereEcho` has no such distinction at all. A composite substituting a field that itself failed to resolve falls back to `""` rather than erroring — never actually exercised by PROCEDURE's own flow, since it rejects the whole file before ever reading `Designation` in that case.
  - **47.3 (Infrastructure, `ImportProfileConfiguration.cs` + migration `20260727215239_AddHeaderRulesToImportProfile`)**: `HeaderFields` (`OwnsMany` → `ImportProfileSheetRuleHeaderFields`, with `Cell` further `OwnsOne`-table-split into the same row as `CellSheet`/`CellRange`) and `HeaderComposites` (`OwnsMany` → `ImportProfileSheetRuleHeaderComposites`), same shadow-`int Id` convention as every other owned collection here. Not yet applied to any real SQL Server database as of this lot.
  - **47.4/47.5 (`DefaultProfileSeeder` + service bascule)**: the seeded PROCEDURE rule now carries `nomMAD`(`M2:O2`, strip)/`revision`(`P2:Q2`)/`dateRev`(`R2:T2`, `"dd/MM/yyyy"`) + the `"Rév {revision} du {dateRev}"` composite; AJT/DIVERS each carry a single `repereEcho`(`N6`) field — all values transcribed verbatim from the code they replace, zero invented. `ProcedureExtractionService`'s constructor swapped `ITextTransformEvaluator` for `IHeaderRuleResolver` (the prefix-strip/composite logic moved fully into the resolver); the whole-file-rejection messages (`"Cellule {range} (repère de l'équipement)..."`) now interpolate the **actual** `HeaderFieldRule.Cell.Range` looked up from the sheet rule (via a new `FindHeaderFieldCellRange` helper) rather than the literal `"M2:O2"`/`"R2:T2"` strings, so a differently-configured profile's rejection messages stay correct too — confirmed identical wording against the default-equivalent profile in both unit and integration tests. `AutresJointsTouchesExtractionService`/`DiversExtractionService` (+ their interfaces) gained a new `reperePrefix` parameter (unused by `repereEcho` today, since it never sets `StripReperePrefix`, but kept for architectural consistency with `ProcedureExtractionService`'s own signature and to let the resolver's `reperePrefix` requirement be satisfied without smuggling an empty string) — `ImportPipelineOrchestrator` now passes `profile.ReperePrefix` through to both. Anti-hardcoding guard-rails added at the service level (not just the resolver level) mirroring the pre-existing `EquipementTypeElementNom` pattern (Lot C1): two profiles with different header coordinates/templates → the service returns each profile's own value, never a constant.
  - **47.6 (non-regression)**: the pre-existing fixture-based integration tests (`ProcedureExtractionServiceIntegrationTests`, `AutresJointsTouchesExtractionServiceIntegrationTests`, `DiversExtractionServiceIntegrationTests`, `ImportPipelineOrchestratorIntegrationTests`, `ImportPipelineOrchestratorLoggingIntegrationTests`, `GenerationPipelineIntegrationTests`, `DefaultProfileSeederPipelineIntegrationTests`, `OxoProcessEndpointTests`) needed their hand-built test profiles' `SheetExtractionRule`s extended with the same header rules the seeder now carries (otherwise `header.Fields["nomMAD"]`/`["repereEcho"]` throws `KeyNotFoundException`) — **zero assertion in any of these files was changed**, only the fixture-construction helpers, satisfying the ticket's explicit "non-régression, sans modification de ses assertions" requirement. Full solution after Lot 047: Domain 300 (was 278, +22: `HeaderFieldRule`/`HeaderCompositeRule`/cross-validation tests)/Application 166 (was 152, +14: `HeaderRuleResolverTests` ×10, 2 anti-hardcoding tests each for Procedure/AJT/Divers)/Infrastructure 174 (was 173, +1 net: 2 new `EfImportProfileStoreTests` round-trip tests, 1 new `DefaultProfileSeederTests` header-rule assertion test)/WebAPI 25 (unaffected)/BlazorAdmin 561 (unaffected — its own `ImportProfileTestTests`/`ExportProfileTestTests` real-pipeline fixtures needed the same header-rule extension plus a new `IHeaderRuleResolver` DI registration in both files' bUnit service collections, but no test count change)/Hosting 6/legacy 9+15 — all green.

- **Lot 048 (Blazor editing of import-profile header rules, done — 2026-07-28, ticket doc `docs/tickets/tickets-tdd-lot-048-edition-blazor-regles-entete-profil.md`)**: closes the gap Lot 047 left open — `HeaderFieldRule`/`HeaderCompositeRule` were persistable but invisible/uneditable from the admin UI. Import-only, per the ticket's own explicit exclusion — `ExportProfile` has no header rules, `ExportProfileEditor.razor` untouched.
  - **48.1 (data-loss fix, done first)**: `SheetRuleForm.Submit()` was passing literal `[], []` for `HeaderFields`/`HeaderComposites` regardless of `InitialRule` — editing *any* sheet rule from the admin (even an unrelated field) silently wiped its header rules, breaking extraction with an unhandled `KeyNotFoundException` on the next run. Fixed by hydrating `_headerFields`/`_headerComposites` from `InitialRule` in `OnInitialized` (mirroring every other sub-list already there) and passing them through `Submit()` — each `HeaderFieldRule`'s `Cell` is rebuilt with `Cell.Sheet` re-derived from the form's current `_sheetName` (decision 2 below), not blindly copied, so a sheet rename stays consistent.
  - **48.2/48.3 (`HeaderFieldRuleForm.razor`/`HeaderCompositeRuleForm.razor`, new)**: same add/edit sub-form shape as `BlockFieldForm.razor` (`IdPrefix`/`Initial…`/`SubmitButtonId`/`CancelButtonId`/`ShowCancel`/`OnCancel`/`OnSubmit<T>`, `try/catch (DomainValidationException)` → `BusinessExceptionLocalizer`). **Decision 2 (Simon, 2026-07-27): the sheet is never entered by the user** — `HeaderFieldRuleForm` only exposes the cell *range*, building a placeholder `DirectCell("__pending__", range)` that `SheetRuleForm` always overwrites with its own current `_sheetName` at submission; the domain still allows cross-sheet reads, but no seeded profile ever needs it and this removes a whole class of data-entry error. A blank `DateFormat` input is converted to `null`, never `""` (a non-null-but-blank `DateFormat` throws `HeaderFieldRule_BlankDateFormat`, which would incorrectly fire for a field the user simply never touched). The strip-prefix checkbox deliberately lives in a plain `form-check`, **not** `form-floating` (`FormFloatingStructureAuditTests` requires a non-empty `placeholder` on every `INPUT` inside a `div.form-floating`, which a checkbox can't satisfy). `HeaderCompositeRuleForm` accepts a template with zero `{placeholder}`s (a literal string) — the domain allows it, `PlaceholderNames()` returns empty, no cross-validation to fail.
  - **48.4 (`SheetRuleForm.razor` integration)**: two new `<div class="card bg-light mb-3">` sections (Header fields / Header composites), same shape and classes as the pre-existing Block fields/Unconditional colonnes/Point rules sections, each with a `block-field-list` of icon-only Modify/Delete rows (`AdminIconMarkup.Pencil`/`.Trash`) plus a permanent add-instance at the bottom. **Decision 1 (Simon): deletion is immediate, no confirmation** — parity with every other `SheetRuleForm` sub-list (the two-step confirm pattern is a `ImportProfileEditor`-card-level-only convention, not a sub-list one). `Submit()` re-derives every `HeaderFieldRule.Cell.Sheet` from `_sheetName` right before constructing the `SheetExtractionRule`, so an unknown-placeholder `DomainRuleViolationException` (`SheetExtractionRule_HeaderCompositeReferencesUnknownField`) — only detectable at this level, never inside the isolated `HeaderCompositeRuleForm` — surfaces as the existing top-of-form `alert-danger role="alert"`, submission abandoned, `OnSubmit` never invoked. **Decision 5 confirmed empirically, not re-wired**: `_hasUnsavedChanges` (Lot 043) is still set only by `HandleAddSheetRule`/`HandleSaveSheetRule` in `ImportProfileEditor` — no new per-mutation hook needed for the two new lists, since they flow through the same `OnSubmit` path every other sub-list already uses.
  - **48.5 (`Shared/KnownHeaderFieldNames.cs`, new)**: a UI-only advisory table (never consulted by extraction) mapping `PROCEDURE`→(`nomMAD`/`revision`/`dateRev`, `Designation`), `AUTRES JOINTS TOUCHES`/`DIVERS`→(`repereEcho`, —) — duplicated sheet-name/field-name literals since `ImportPipelineOrchestrator`'s own constants are `private` and `ProcedureHeaderFieldNames`/`SharedHeaderFieldNames` (Application) are already referenced by BlazorAdmin. `SheetRuleForm` renders a non-blocking `alert-warning role="alert"` (`#{IdPrefix}header-well-known-names-warning`) listing any missing expected name for the current `_sheetName`, ordinal/case-sensitive comparison (matching the real resolver's `Dictionary<string,...>` lookup) — submitting despite the warning still works, exactly the "flag, don't block" instruction.
  - **48.6 (`ImportProfileEditor.razor` read-only visibility, added to scope by the ticket's own D9)**: the sheet-rule card's `<details>` gained two more conditional blocks (header fields: name+range; header composites: name+template), and `ImportProfileEditor_SheetRuleDetailsSummary` grew from 2 to 4 template arguments (`{0} unconditional colonnes, {1} conditional rules, {2} header fields, {3} header composites`) — without this, header rules existed only in edit mode, defeating "configurable without a developer".
  - **48.7 (localization, both directions)**: (a) new `ImportProfileEditor_*` UI keys (headings/labels/placeholders/hints/buttons/warning), EN+FR, reusing `_ModifyButton`/`_DeleteButton`/`_SaveChangesButton`/`_CancelButton` verbatim rather than duplicating. (b) **7 `DomainErrorMessages` keys Lot 047 never added** (`HeaderFieldRule_EmptyName`/`_BlankDateFormat`, `HeaderCompositeRule_EmptyName`/`_EmptyTemplate`, `SheetExtractionRule_HeaderCompositeReferencesUnknownField`, `DirectCell_EmptySheet`/`_InvalidRange`) — without them `BusinessExceptionLocalizer` would have displayed the raw key name verbatim to the user. Verified by a **real** (non-mocked) `.resx`-backed `IStringLocalizer<DomainErrorMessages>` test, `tests/ExcelETL.Application.Tests/Exceptions/DomainErrorMessagesHeaderRuleLocalizationTests.cs` (a thin `ResourceManager` wrapper, since the Application test project only references `Microsoft.Extensions.Localization.Abstractions`, not a concrete implementation) — asserts every key's localized message differs from the raw key name in both `en` and `fr`, the one guarantee that catches a forgotten key.
  - Tests: `ImportProfileEditorTests.cs` (+27 new `[Fact]`/`[Theory]` methods, one `[Theory]` expanding into 3 cases → +29 test executions, covering all sub-form/integration/warning/read-only scenarios above including a red-first pair for 48.1 predating any UI wiring) plus one existing test's assertion updated in place (`SheetRuleSublistDetails_WithEmptySublist_ClickingShowsCoherentEmptyState`, for the new 4-part empty-state message). `DomainErrorMessagesHeaderRuleLocalizationTests.cs` (+14, Application.Tests). Full solution after Lot 048: Domain 300 (unaffected)/Application 180 (was 166, +14)/Infrastructure 174 (unaffected)/WebAPI 25 (unaffected)/BlazorAdmin 590 (was 561, +29)/Hosting 6/legacy 9+15 — all green.
  - **Hors périmètre, per the ticket**: any Domain/Application/Infrastructure/migration/seed change beyond 48.1's Blazor-only fix; `ExportProfileEditor.razor` parity; making the extraction services tolerant of a missing header name (`TryGetValue` instead of the indexer) — flagged as "047-bis", not this lot; a template-authoring assist (autocomplete/preview); cleanup of the pre-existing duplicated inline Pencil/Trash/Check/X SVGs in `SheetRuleForm`'s older sub-lists (only the *new* markup uses `AdminIconMarkup` — the audit-flagged existing duplication stays a separate, deferred ticket).

- **Lot 049 (`/Account/ForcePasswordChange` was unreachable — "Introuvable", done — 2026-07-28, ticket doc `docs/tickets/tickets-tdd-lot-049-correction-page-force-password-change-introuvable.md`)**: Lot 045's forced-password-change page shipped green in bUnit yet displayed the NotFound page in the browser, locking any admin-created account out of the app entirely (go-live blocker). **The root cause was `App.razor`, not the page**: `<Routes @rendermode="InteractiveServer" />` was a *constant*, so every page under `Components/Account/` — which `Components/Account/_Imports.razor` marks `[ExcludeFromInteractiveRouting]` — was prerendered as an interactive component. Fixed by computing the render mode per request: `HttpContext.AcceptsInteractiveRouting() ? InteractiveServer : null` (`RazorComponentsEndpointHttpContextExtensions`, `Microsoft.AspNetCore.Components.Endpoints`).
  - **`AcquireRenderModeForPage` does not exist in .NET 10** (checked against the installed `Microsoft.AspNetCore.App` 10.0.10 — the string is absent from every shared-framework assembly), despite being what the ticket and various docs assume. `AcceptsInteractiveRouting()` is the real API. Don't reintroduce the other name.
  - **Mechanism, measured not assumed** (49.0 diagnostic, full write-up in the ticket doc): the framework's `Router` genuinely **removes** `[ExcludeFromInteractiveRouting]` pages from its *interactive* route table — a bUnit test rendering `Router` with the app's own `AppAssembly`/`NotFoundPage` resolves `/Account/Login`, `/Account/Register` **and** `/Account/ForcePasswordChange` to `NotFound`, while `/import-profiles` resolves normally (`InteractiveRoutingExclusionTests`, kept as a permanent characterization test). So the server rendered the page correctly (`200`, real form, real `<title>`), then the circuit booted over it, found no route and replaced the body with `NotFoundPage`; `<HeadOutlet />` has no render mode so it stayed static, which is why the tab title of the *real* page survived over a NotFound body. `/Account/Login` and `/Account/Register` carried the identical defect latently. No HTTP error code was ever involved — `UseStatusCodePagesWithReExecute` was never triggered, and `BlazorDisableThrowNavigationException` was verified non-causal (left in place as known debt, along with the render-mode-less `HeadOutlet`).
  - **The lesson, propagated to `docs/conventions/recommandations-tickets-tdd.md` §6**: a bUnit test never proves a page is reachable — it renders the component directly, short-circuiting routing, render mode and the whole HTTP pipeline. Any ticket making routing/navigation/redirection a requirement needs a `WebApplicationFactory<Program>` test doing a real HTTP request. **But that is necessary, not sufficient**: an `HttpClient` never opens the SignalR circuit, so a defect that only appears once the circuit starts stays invisible — the request must be paired with an assertion on the render mode actually served (absence/presence of the `"type":"server"` interactive-component marker in the response body). Verified the hard way here: the 49.5 end-to-end journey test **passed with the defect deliberately restored**, and only became meaningful once that marker assertion was added to it.
  - **First `WebApplicationFactory<Program>` harness for `ExcelETL.BlazorAdmin`** (the repo had none — see the pre-Lot-034 note about `ProfileSeeding:Enabled` having no host-level coverage). Two files, each with its own colocated factory setup per this repo's no-shared-test-helper convention: `tests/ExcelETL.BlazorAdmin.Tests/Account/ForcePasswordChangeHttpTests.cs` (49.1 + 49.5) and `tests/ExcelETL.BlazorAdmin.Tests/Components/AppRenderModeHttpTests.cs` (49.2's two-sided non-regression: Account pages static, admin pages interactive). Setup that any future BlazorAdmin host-level test should copy: `UseEnvironment("Development")`; `Serilog:EnableMsSqlServerSink=false`; `Database:AutoMigrate=false`; `IdentitySeeding:Enabled=false`; **`ProfileSeeding:Enabled=false`** (now genuinely exercised, closing that old coverage gap); `OxoApiTestClient:BaseUrl`/`ApiKey` set to dummies because `Program.cs` fails fast without them; and **both** `ApplicationIdentityDbContext` registrations swapped to EF InMemory (`AddDbContext` for `AddEntityFrameworkStores` *and* the Scoped `AddDbContextFactory` for `IUserRepository`) onto the same database name, plus `ExcelEtlDbContext`. Signing in over HTTP means replaying the Blazor SSR form's hidden fields verbatim — both `__RequestVerificationToken` **and** `_handler` (the `FormName`) — and `/Account/Logout` is a plain minimal API POST needing `__RequestVerificationToken` + `returnUrl`.
  - **49.4 (`PasswordChangeGuard.razor`)**: `AllowedPathPrefixes` gained `not-found` and `error`. An error page was previously a redirect target for any flagged user — a latent redirect loop (masked only by `BlazorDisableThrowNavigationException`) that also hid the very error message needed to diagnose a failure. Both paths are reached by pipeline re-execution (`UseStatusCodePagesWithReExecute("/not-found")`, `UseExceptionHandler("/Error")`), so the guard fired on them systematically. 4 bUnit tests (navigation + fresh render, each error page), red before the fix; the `/users` non-regression was already covered and stayed green.
  - **49.3 has no content**: branch B (server error code) was formally ruled out in 49.0, so there was nothing to fix there.
  - Full solution after Lot 049: Domain 300/Application 180/Infrastructure 174/WebAPI 25/Hosting 6/legacy 9+15 (all unaffected) / BlazorAdmin **609** (was 590; +19) — all green. **The manual `UPDATE AspNetUsers SET RequirePasswordChangeOnFirstLogin = 0` workaround from the ticket header is no longer needed.**
  - **Not verified in a real browser this session** (no running dev server/admin credentials available) — but unlike Lot 045, the reachability claim now rests on real HTTP requests plus a render-mode assertion rather than on component-level rendering alone.
- **Lot 050 (identité de connexion explicite, unicité de l'e-mail, liste utilisateurs lisible — done, 2026-07-28, ticket doc `docs/tickets/tickets-tdd-lot-050-identite-connexion-username-unicite-email-role-visible.md`)**: reopens Lot 044's `UserName = email` decision — `Login.razor` authenticates by user name (confirmed at 50.0), never by email, and the 3 seeded accounts (`SLB`/`J2M`/`JPN`) never followed the email convention anyway.
  - **50.0 investigation, both stop-points cleared**: the 3 seeded accounts (config `AdminSeedUsers`) satisfy D1/D2 as-is — no seed/validator adjustment was needed to avoid locking out the app's own admin on a fresh database. `/Account/Register` **is reachable** (`[AllowAnonymous]`, `@page "/Account/Register"`) and still hardcodes `UserName = Input.Email` — **flagged here, not fixed**: a public sign-up page on an admin app defaulting to an now-invalid (contains `@`, disallowed under D1) username is a security/product question for Simon, not a format bug for this lot to silently patch. `UserManager.SetUserNameAsync` was confirmed (via ASP.NET Core Identity's own documented behavior) to persist immediately (calls `UpdateUserAsync` internally) — avoided in 50.3 in favor of assigning `UserName`/`Email`/`FirstName`/`LastName` directly followed by one single `UpdateAsync`, so a rejected field never leaves a partially-written user.
  - **50.1 (`ApplicationUserValidator`, `src/ExcelETL.Infrastructure/Identity/`)**: a new `IUserValidator<ApplicationUser>` (`AddUserValidator<ApplicationUserValidator>()`, additive alongside Identity's own default validator, never replacing it) enforces D1 (UserName 3-30) and D2 (FirstName/LastName 2-50, **no character-set restriction** — `Le Becq`/`Jean-Marie`/`O'Brien`/`N'Diaye` all pass, deliberately not extended by symmetry from D1's character set, which would reject 2 of the 3 real seeded accounts). D1's character set itself (`AllowedUserNameCharacters` — letters/digits/`_`/`.`, `-`/`@`/`+` removed from Identity's own default) is a separate native `IdentityOptions.User` option, set once in `ExcelETL.BlazorAdmin/Program.cs`'s `AddIdentity<...>` call — proven wired via a real `UserManager` + EF Core InMemory integration test (`UserNameCharacterSetIdentityIntegrationTests`), not just declared, since a mocked `UserManager` (used everywhere else in this test project) would never exercise it. New `InfrastructureMessages` keys (`UserNameLengthInvalid`/`FirstNameLengthInvalid`/`LastNameLengthInvalid`/`UserNameRequired`), same `Localize(code, args)` convention as `LocalizedIdentityErrorDescriber`. The seed-conformance test (`ApplicationUserValidatorTests.ValidateAsync_SeedUserValues_SatisfyTheValidator`) reads the real `AdminSeedUsers` section from `ExcelETL.BlazorAdmin/appsettings.json` at test time (`RealSeedUsersLoader`, plain `System.Text.Json`, no `Configuration.Json`/`.Binder` package added to the test project) rather than hand-copying the values — the permanent guard-rail against the "seeded admin silently locked out on a fresh database" risk this lot's own ticket calls out as its central danger.
  - **50.2/50.3 (`IUserManagementService`, Infrastructure `UserManagementService`)**: `CreateUserAsync` gained an explicit, independent `userName` parameter (first, never derived from `email` again) — blank/whitespace fails before any `CreateAsync` call (`UserNameRequired`). New `UpdateUserAsync(userId, userName, email, firstName, lastName)` (deliberately **not** a change to `IUserRepository.UpdateProfileAsync`, which also backs `Profile.razor`'s self-service editing and stays untouched, no username field, out of this lot's scope) — one single `UpdateAsync` call persists all four fields atomically (Identity's own `ValidateUserAsync`, including `ApplicationUserValidator` + `RequireUniqueEmail` + `AllowedUserNameCharacters`, runs first; on failure `Store.UpdateAsync` is never reached). `UpdateSecurityStampAsync` is called only when the user name actually changed, compared via a plain `OrdinalIgnoreCase` string comparison (not `UserManager.NormalizeName`, whose mocked-by-default behavior in this test project's `Mock<UserManager<ApplicationUser>>` returns `null` unless explicitly stubbed — `OrdinalIgnoreCase` achieves the same practical case-insensitive effect as Identity's own upper-invariant normalizer without depending on Moq's `CallBase`).
  - **50.4 (D5, applicative half)**: `options.User.RequireUniqueEmail = true` set alongside `AllowedUserNameCharacters` in the same `AddIdentity` call (the only host registering Identity — confirmed, `ExcelETL.WebAPI` has none). Proven via real `UserManager` + EF InMemory (`RequireUniqueEmailIdentityIntegrationTests`): duplicate email rejected (`DuplicateEmail`), case-insensitive duplicates rejected, and the classic false-positive guard (**a user keeping its own unchanged email must still succeed** — its absence would make every user edit impossible in production).
  - **50.5 (migration `20260728004819_AddUniqueEmailIndexAndNameLengthsToApplicationUser`, `ApplicationIdentityDbContext.OnModelCreating`, called **after** `base.OnModelCreating`)**: `EmailIndex` redefined `IsUnique().HasFilter("[NormalizedEmail] IS NOT NULL")` (the filter is not optional — SQL Server treats multiple `NULL`s as equal under an unfiltered unique index) plus `FirstName`/`LastName` `HasMaxLength(50)` (schema-level safety net only, **not** a duplicate of D2's user-facing rule, which stays 50.1's alone). A model-level test (`ApplicationIdentityDbContextModelTests`) checks the index/filter/lengths are declared — explicitly **not** claimed to prove the index/filter's real effect, since the InMemory provider ignores both; that needs the real-SQL-Server essai à blanc mentioned in the ticket. Not yet applied to any real SQL Server database as of this lot.
  - **50.6/50.7/50.8/50.9 (`Users.razor`)**: a `#create-user-username-input`/`#edit-user-username-input-{id}` (+ `-card-{id}`) field, first in both forms, prefilled on edit, plain-presence client validation only (D1/D2/uniqueness stay server-only, existing lot-F convention) plus a short `Users_UserNameHelp` informational text (3-30 chars, letters/digits/`_`/`.`, "this is the sign-in identifier") — never a validation, since the rule isn't otherwise deducible from the page. `UserSummary` gained `FirstName`/`LastName` (`UserRepository.GetAllAsync`'s projection extended) so the list needs no per-row `GetByIdAsync`; both rendered in table and card (V2 content-identity test extended, not duplicated). A read-only `#user-role-badge-{id}` (`-card-{id}` for the mobile card, distinct id per the established V2 convention since both templates coexist in the DOM) shows a `badge` for Admin accounts and plain text for standard ones — **never color alone** (WCAG 1.4.1), reuses the exact `_adminUserIds` list already fetched once per render for the "last Admin" guard (44.3) — a dedicated test proves `GetAdminUserIdsAsync` is called exactly once for N≥3 rows, an N+1 regression guard. The `Id` column/GUID is gone from both templates (identifiers stay carried by the row buttons' own HTML ids); the table gained `table-sm`; the 3 row-action buttons (desktop table) are now wrapped in a `.right-aligned-actions` div, matching the treatment the mobile card already had.

- **Lot 051 (retrait de l'inscription publique, done — 2026-07-28, ticket doc `docs/tickets/tickets-tdd-lot-051-retrait-inscription-publique.md`)**: closes the gap Lot 050's own 50.0 investigation flagged — `Register.razor` always built `UserName = Input.Email`, and D1's restricted username character set (Lot 050) made `@` invalid, so the page had started **systematically rejecting its own sign-ups** with an incomprehensible Identity error. Decided with Simon: removal, not repair — repairing it would reintroduce public self-registration, which defeats Lot 044's own purpose (admin-only account creation, so the admin never has to share personal credentials).
  - **51.0 investigation confirmed the periphery is exactly what the audit inventory said and nothing more**: no `ForgotPassword`/`ConfirmEmail`/`ResendEmailConfirmation`/`ExternalLogin` page exists anywhere in this project (the Identity scaffold was pruned from the start) — `Login.razor`'s bottom-of-form link block held exactly one link, to `Account/Register`, confirmed by direct reading rather than assumed from the standard template's usual three. No `IEmailSender<ApplicationUser>`/`IdentityNoOpEmailSender` registration exists in `Program.cs` — nothing to remove at 51.4.
  - **51.1**: `RegisterRemovalHttpTests.cs` (new, `WebApplicationFactory<Program>`, same colocated-setup convention as Lots 049/050) written and run **red** before deletion (`Get_Register_WithoutAuthentication_ReturnsNotFound` failed against the live 200-returning page), then `Register.razor` deleted. **The ticket's own literal 404 assumption for an unauthenticated request turned out wrong on measurement** — a fresh, deliberate re-application of the Lot 049 lesson ("measure, don't assume") to a case the ticket itself got wrong: `Program.cs`'s global `AuthorizationOptions.FallbackPolicy` (`RequireAuthenticatedUser()`) gates `MapRazorComponents<App>()`'s catch-all endpoint itself, so an unauthenticated request to any unmapped route (confirmed generic by probing an unrelated nonsense path side by side, identical response) never reaches the Blazor Router at all — it's challenged by cookie authentication first, `302` to `/Account/Login?ReturnUrl=...`. The generated-file count and the last-generation date share **one** `GetSummaryAsync` call — a `GeneratedFileArchiveStore` failure marks both indicators `Unavailable` together, rather than querying the store twice for two indicators backed by the same aggregate.
